## Next-step limits
- Il2CppDumper recovered the method map and offsets (metadata version 31), but it does not restore original C# method bodies.
- The listed RVA/file offsets identify native ARM64 code locations for a next reverse-engineering pass; offsets alone are not a safe gameplay patch.
- The original playable scene files, transforms, colliders, maps and level layouts remain unchanged.
